HWbpgYiQN80;;;Katamari on the Rocks - Katamari Damacy REROLL;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/Katamari%20on%20the%20Rocks%20-%20Katamari%20Damacy%20REROLL ;;;The rip is an homage to the rips "Katamari on the Rocks - Katamari Damacy" and "Lost Woods (Complete Version) - The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time". Similar to the aforementioned rips, it is a dense mashup medley using various sources, with this rip being comprised almost entirely of channel memes that became popular after the latter rip. This rip also incorporates various arranged instrumentals. 4zDLt6pSts4;;;News of Jay's Death - The Ignition Factor;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/News%20of%20Jay's%20Death%20-%20The%20Ignition%20Factor ;;;This rip continues the "e∩Ω" story arc, taking place after "It's time for a feast...". The rip's title references Jay Eazy, and in turn the events of "Golden Ratio - e". At the start, the screen moves from the game's thumbnail to the inside of Hell. An "e" portal opens and both Gegagedigedagedago and the roasted chicken from "Gegagedigedagedago 5" by Gchmch step out of it. From 0:08 to 0:37, the advertised track's melody is mashed up with the vocals of @raizoff's "Cotton Eye Joe" cover while Gegagedigedagedago and the Roasted Chicken character traverse the depths of Hell. Additionally, several characters and references to other rips make cameo appearances during this trip:  Jerry from Undertale can be seen at 0:10-0:11 From 0:12 to 0:18, both CraftyCorn from Poppy Playtime and a Young Sheldon / Walter White edit, which references the "Young Sheldon Ending" meme, appear in the backgroud. Between 0:19 and 0:24, the Josh Hutcherson / Omega Nugget edit, circular chain, Last Bible III logo and "AI Rick Laughing" videos from "Senatus - Last Bible III" can be seen at the top of the screen. Jack-in-the-Box Zombie from Plants vs. Zombies appears in the background between 0:25 and 0:31. Gegagedigedagedago eventually reaches a large hole at 0:38 as the background music stops. Gegagedigedagedago pulls out an "e" tile, which first appeared in "Gameplay - 2048", and punctures a hole in it with his knife featuring a ROBLOX Man-Face, which first appeared in "Tsurupettan (Beta Mix) - Umineko no Naku Koro ni". This leads to the simple continued fraction form of "e" bursting out of the tile, creating a staircase that Gegagedigedagedago and the Roasted Chicken character use to slide down the hole. At the bottom of the hole, the rip "The Infinite Hole - The Stanley Parable: Ultra Deluxe" plays, as they come across The numberphile's ghost, who greets them with the "Hey" lyric from "Mega Man". Gegagegedigedagedago nods to The numberphile and exchanges a handshake, with the latter walking up to the fraction staircase. The numberphile touches the staircase, which causes it to move towards The numberphile. The infinite staircase collides with him, which causing him to glow white before being fully revived. The numberphile thanks the duo for reviving him, and drops another "e" tile for them, before jumping away. The Roasted Turkey grabs the "e" tile and throws it to open a portal as the fart sound associated with Brainless Kitty plays. The rip ends with Gegagedigedagedago and the Roasted Chicken entering the portal as the screen becomes black.;;;minindo, Myeauxyoozi

OtsryhMHEhY;;;King of the Creatures - Sam & Max Hit the Road;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/King%20of%20the%20Creatures%20-%20Sam%20%26%20Max%20Hit%20the%20Road ;;;This is a "cover" of the advertised track, with the lead singer performing in an impersonation of the game's main villain Conroy Bumpus's voice over a MIDI arrangement of the track's instrumental. Starting at the end of the first verse, the "cover" becomes a spoof of "The Mine Song" from LazyTown sung in-character as Bumpus. The "bigfoot creature" and "circus feature" in the spoof lyrics refer to two characters in Sam & Max Hit the Road – Bruno the Bigfoot and Trixie the Giraffe-Necked Girl from Scranton – whom Bumpus has kidnapped and forced to play in his backing band. b2XVpZGxirQ;;;The First Date - A Date with Boykisser!;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/The%20First%20Date%20-%20A%20Date%20with%20Boykisser! ;;;There doesn't exist a track named "The First Date" in A Date with Boykisser!. Instead, this rip is fake gameplay of a fake visual novel-style "Choose Your Own Adventure" game focus on Boykisser. The rip starts with the player arriving at the fancy restaurant, and attends to the waitress - being the cat Ethel, associated with the "oo ee a e a" meme - for a front desk reservation. The waitress then guides the player to the table, while "U ii a io ui ii a io #keşfetolmaduası🤲📿🧕" by @krexarpect plays when "walk-spin", and lets the player sit there patiently while wait for their date to arrive. Not too long after, Boykisser arrives for the date, saying that he was late due to "got too caught up at home", in which the player calms him down to not worry about it, and just had arrived at the right time. Boykisser says to be his first time on a date, and feeling a bit nervous, specially for "being around with a cute boy like the player". The player blushes, resulting into Boykisser flirting with him for a bit, and later, he asks for the player if wanted to know anything about himself, in which the player asks for what kind-of music he likes. Boykisser responds to like the "Silly Music" genre that makes him go "chipi chapa" around. The player doesn't understand what he meant, but find his quirkiness kind-of charming, and then talks about their personal music taste too, in which Boykisser afirms to not heard about those genres due to not being well versed into it, and promises to give it a go in some time. The waitress Ethel comes by to see if they're ready to order anything, in which the player asks for Boykisser what he wants, and happy responds salmon, in which makes the player and Ethel a little startled and confused by the sudden outbust respond, in which Boykisser says sorry for it, and saying that he was just hungry and likes to eat fish. Ethel then writes down the orders and goes heads to the kitchen, while the player was a little confuse and ask for what happen in there. Boykisser responds that he cant help but be really excited, as since he responds for him ""being a silly cat". Leaving the situation aside, the player asks if he knows or hangs around with other felines, in which Boykisser responds excitelly that does, saying that he's friends with Kapi - PaperKitty's character and main opponent in the Friday Night Funkin' mod, VS. KAPI - Arcade Showdown - and mentions that usually hang out at the arcade together, and even invites the player in case want to join in if it's into playing games. After this, Boykisser mentions to be friends with Eek! The Cat, in which by coincidence, mentions that both shares the same favorite song. Boykisser then says that he has non-feline friends as well, mentioning two characters that are best friends with him: Pomni from The Amazing Digital Circus and Gegagedigedagedago, with both images of them appearing in transparent on the left. Boykisser says that the player likely knows them, saying that "they're pretty famous around here", and admit saying that didn't been places as such because of them, and saying that Gegagedigedagedago gets really awkward, and Pomni is usually pretty timid and confused, but saying that they're really kind people when get to know them more. Player recalls to know Gegagedigedagedago, as mentioning that "he's a well known hero around these parts", while also heard about Pomni, questioning if was the girl that "randomly showed up one day". Boykisser then continues mentioning having a roommate fox called Lyan - Mauzymice's boyfriend character - in which mentions being confused with himself. The waitress Ethel comes with the food (a salmon with ROBLOX Man Face attached into it) and drinks that has been ordered, and she asks if wanted anything else. The player responds no, but when tried to ask with Boykisser, he quickly eats the sushi, and decides to request for more salmons in excitement. The player notices it and hopes that somehow Boykisser pays for the foods, as since themselves can't afford it. Ethan then goes on the kitchen and comes back to give more salmon to Boykisser,  which he eats all in one bite while World's Loudest Orgasm's scream plays follow by the Heavy's "nom" sound from Team Fortress 2.  In excitement, Boykisser asks for more before player decides to calm him down and convince him that he's getting too much. Boykisser apologies in advance, saying that he got "a little carried away" and that he "was in a trance". He quickly thanks the player to avoid to picking up as since he doesn't know to control himself when comes to fish food, in which the player tell them to no need and that they understand, and asks if he'd like to split the payment check, in which Boykisser says to not need, as since he got money for his portion, and says in regards to picking up more fish later, in which the player mentions that he can't guarantee for more fish about it. After this, the player asks if Boykisser wanted to go for somewhere else for the rest of the night, in which he accepts, but depends over the transportation that they're gonna take, as since he didn't brought a car. He asks if the player wanted to call a taxi, or wanted to walk together, follow by two carded videos to choose: "Taxi to the City - A Date with Boykisser!" and "Peaceful Walk in the Park - A Date with Boykisser!". The rip ends afterwards.  References[] Throughout from the entire rip, a single song plays in the background, being "It's Raining Somewhere Else (Alpha Mix) - Undertale". T1VkW4nk_Iw;;;Main Theme - Poker Night at the Inventory;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/Main%20Theme%20-%20Poker%20Night%20at%20the%20Inventory ;;;This is an original live-action skit created by several rippers during Super MAGFest 2025. It depicts a tournament of a poker-like card game, played mostly with flash cards. The skit begins with Franklin and his friend Carl John Smith IV entering a hotel room where the tournament is held, in which there are several people excitedly betting on the ongoing match. Mark, a highly skilled poker player, defeats Bartholomew, and is slated to face Gork in the final match of the tournament. Carl and Franklin watch the match eagerly, although Franklin's inexperience with the game's rules causes him a lot of confusion. Mark begins the match by playing the "splash", "drink", and "frog" cards as his first hand, followed by playing a "snail" card. Gork then plays a "swim" card, which Mark counters by flipping his "snail" card, causing Gork to shout angrily in disbelief. As the spectators comprehend the sudden twist of events, Gork makes a plea to the Dealer to rig the game in his favor, which Dealer refuses. As the game continues, Mark, now overconfident from his imminent victory, taunts at Gork to forfeit the match. Gork responds to Mark's mockery by mentioning that him losing the game would result in his family's death, which Mark bluntly rebuffs by playing a "fish" card. Seeing that Franklin is struggling to follow the game's events, Carl attempts to explain to him Mark's strategies up to that point. Unfortunately, his detailed explanations only serve to further overwhelm and confuse Franklin. With the match nearing its conclusion, Gork is projected to lose the tournament to Mark. In the face of this, Gork appears to forfeit the match, tearfully walking away from Mark. However, just as Dealer is about to call the match in Mark's favor, Gork suddenly retaliates with a "THE LITERAL ROCK", with all the spectators looking on in awe. With this new revelation, Gork is declared the winner of the tournament, with all of his spectators cheering for his victory. As Carl and Franklin leave the hotel room, Franklin, utterly bewildered, questions Carl about the events that he witnessed.;;;see § Credits

lpC8lVQc6nk;;;PlayStation Startup () - Console/BIOS Music;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/PlayStation%20Startup%20()%20-%20Console%2FBIOS%20Music ;;;This is based on Hitogata, an urban legend about a Japanese train safety PSA. The rip starts with the PlayStation startup that is edited to appear to be playing on a TV that also gets cutoff before it fully ends. At 0:18, a disclaimer in Japanese appears, warning about the software still being in development and issues with disk may occur. At 0:26, the game in question is shown to be the PlayStation port of Densha de GO! with the games opening cutscene and title screen shown. At 0:55, the player tries to select a train, however three of the options are shown to still be under development, leaving the top left one as the only option. After viewing the regular instructions about how to play the game, gameplay proceeds normally at first, with the player exiting the first station and heading to the next station. At 3:02, the player applies the brakes too late, causing them to miss the platform and lose all their bonus time. As in the real game, a red train icon reading "Time is up" appears. At 3:26, gameplay restarts but begins to deviate heavily; almost all of the HUD elements are corrupted except for the speedometer, which constantly switches between 0km/h and 130km/h, the sky is pitch black, and the tracks are completely straight. As well, there is almost nothing else surrounding the track, with only the ground, telegraph poles, a couple of houses, and station platforms present. The ground is also shown to be disappearing right as it passes the screen on the lower half. At 4:45, the player approaches the station, where a person is present on the track. At 4:50, the train collides with the person, the screen goes black, a crashing sound is heard, and the train icon has blood splatter added over it. At 4:55, the distance of the train stopping is shown with it stopping right at the accident, blood surrounding the crash, and no other HUD elements appearing. At 5:01, the "Time is up" screen appears again, followed by the game over screen. At 5:12, a recreation of Hitogata is shown with the background consisting of a train station, with the text shown changed to "パスト/ザー/デヅレー/フャー/ウンダースタンディング" (which roughly translates to "Past the desire for understanding"). At 5:40, an "ADDRESS ERROR" screen is shown before the rip ends.;;;Netyasha Roozi
HtpX5hhQV7c;;;A History of Boxing - Frog Fractions;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/A%20History%20of%20Boxing%20-%20Frog%20Fractions ;;;The commentary in the advertised track is replaced with the entirety of the commentary from "Car Doors of Time ~ Side Story - Mr Rental: The Video Game". i6ER8Dgc1WQ;;;Car Doors of Time ~ Side Story - Mr Rental: The Video Game;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/Car%20Doors%20of%20Time%20~%20Side%20Story%20-%20Mr%20Rental%3A%20The%20Video%20Game ;;;Similar to "Behind the Scenes - Mr Rental: The Video Game", this rip is a lore explanation video narrated by JoeBro, explaining the full story of the PAINT Branch of The Susie ARG.   2B and 9S[] After the events of NieR: Automata's true ending, 2B and 9S live happily ever after in their home dimension. Elsewhere, The Voice Inside Your Head decides to summon 2B using the Figment Transportation Device, planning on making her watch Haltmann and keep him in line. only for a Garlic Taco portal to appear. As 2B is about to be sucked in, 9S jumps in the way, falling into the portal and landing in the Central Canon Continuum. He lands in SiIva's house and is apprehended by The Voice's troops. Despite the setback, The Voice recruits 9S anyway, assuming the latter had hacked the Figment Transportation Device to summon himself in 2B's place. In secret, 9S tries to reverse the Figment Transportation Device's programming to send him home, only to be caught. He launches the undercooked program, causing the device to malfunction and send him to Mundus from Skyrim. Elsewhere, 2B is picked by Eggnog and sent to the Microsoft Paint universe. He instructs 2B to find the Crappily Drawn Car and never stop driving it. This leads into 2B's cameo appearance in "Opening Scene - Waluigi's Tacostand", which happens during Mr. Rent's flashback to Mr. Own at The Fucking Store, as well as "Story Mode - Mr Rental: The Video Game" and "Story Mode - Looney Tunes", which reuse the NBG's initial concepts of the car being able to travel between both worlds, with the addition of it being able to travel back 1 minute into the past when it swaps universes. 2B steals Mr. Own's car, rams into him, sending them both to the Looney Tunes universe. 2B loses the car, and steals Mr. Rental's, running him over and sending them both back to the Microsoft Paint universe. 2B loses the car, and the loop repeats until the multiverse redirects 2B to the CCC to avoid a paradox. Both cars merge into one, becoming a Figment and losing its power. This leads to the events of "The Life and Times of Wade L.D. - A SiIvaGunner Christmas Comeback Crisis Side Story", where 2B steals the Garlic Taco from Haltmann Works Company. She seeks out Wade L.D. to help protect her from the police force hunting her down, eventually respecting Wade and offering to help him escape. The police end up chasing them across dimensions, with Wade sacrificing his life to help 2B escape. Elsewhere, 9S finds the Elder Scroll and uses its power to tear through space-time to send a message. This message reaches Eggnog, who finds 2B during the events of "Ransomware - Undertale 2" and sends her to Mundus. 2B and 9S reunite and use the Elder Scroll's power to return home. 9S broadcasts a follow-up message thanking Eggnog for his help.  Gegagedi and Numberphile[] After the events of "Golden Ratio - e" and the e∩Ω arc as a whole, Gegagedi was in an emotionally numb state after killing Numberphile. In "The Problem - e", he and his friends, still in the Coordinate Plane, looked through Numberphile's computer, noticing he had received an e-mail from a yet-to-be-revealed Susie Haltmann, warning him that he would die. The computer then got another e-mail; 9S' thank you message to Eggnog. A pop-up appeared, stating that a T-coordinate needed to be entered to forward it. Susie, who had perceived everything and was telling this narrative through the ARG, knew that Gegaedi would find the message and instructed him to fill in the correct coordinates, using her e-mail to Numberphile as clues, as well as the message in the description. With help from the audience, in "The Solution - e", Gegagedi deciphered the date the e-mail was supposed to be sent as January 31, 2017 at 9:30AM PST, translating to 1485883800 in T-coordinates. The message was sent back in time to that date for Eggnog to find, which is what causes him to go find 2B and help her in the first place. Seeing the mention of a car, Eggnog knew it was the Crappily Drawn Car from the Microsoft Paint universe due to his status as an anomaly. Back in the Coordinate Plane, Susie sent another e-mail to Gegagedi, encouraging him to forgive himself and tell him it might not be too late to save Numberphile, breaking him out of his stupor and leading into "It's time for a feast...", where he is filled with newfound hope.  Mr. Rent and Waluigi[] During 2B's travels across the multiverse, she went to the Kirby Fighters universe and got a Garlic Taco from Waluigi. This sent her to the Kirby Fighters Gameboy universe, leading to the events of "Main Theme - Kirby Fighters". Grey Kirby jumps through the open portal 2B left, going to the colorful universe and moving in with his counterpart. In "Main Theme - Kirby Fighters 2", the two Kirbys go to Waluigi's Tacostand. Grey Kirby samples one of Waluigi's tacos, which is so hot that he spits fire at him. Angered, Waluigi picks a fight, only for Pink Kirby to snatch a garlic taco, which opens a wormhole and sends Waluigi to the CCC. But, due to the Garlic Taco's time-warping properties, he goes to after 2B first arrived in the CCC. Mr. Rent, who was in the backseat of the Crappily Drawn Car when 2B stole it, was also sent to the CCC. As he got out of the car, Waluigi fell onto him. The resulting collision energy causes Mr. Rent and Waluigi's memories to be rewritten, making it seem like they've known each other for years. The two move in together and Waluigi gets a job working for El Bean, leading into "Opening Scene - Waluigi's Tacostand". Waluigi creates a taco for the "first time", leading to a portal opening. El Bean fires Waluigi and covers the portal with a blanket. El Bean made his rounds across Grandiose City, stopping by SiIva's house, while in the past, The Voice was attempting to summon 2B. His instance of Parsec ate his input to summon 2B, but coincidentally, the garlic taco portal from El Bean's truck was connected to the NieR universe. Pushing 2B out of the way of the portal, 9S was brought into the CCC at the truck's future point in space (SiIva's house) but at a previous point in time, before the taco was created.  Conclusion[] Despite everything having no consequences to the main story, the narrator concludes that it's okay to have fun with a story and not have it be consistent. Since the main story goes against having anomalies to avoid plot contrivances, stories and worlds away from it can have all the fun with anomalies instead. Art is about what is taken away from it and enjoying making it.;;;JoeBro, ackrostation, Grambam36

OQ00Vb8-JLo;;;Overworld () - Super Mario Bros. 2;;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/Overworld%20()%20-%20Super%20Mario%20Bros.%202 ;;;The video is presented in the format of "Thread Simulators", with the advertised track playing in the background as text-to-speech voices read social media posts. In this case, the video shows 4chan posts dated October 11, 2018, the day after the Unregistered HyperCam 2 Takeover. The first post is a response to a supposed leak of the cast of the King for Another Day Tournament as seen in the image which led to the discovery of this video (itself a reference to "The Grinch Leak"), and the user posts their own "actual leak", which refers to the fake contestants seen in the image. At 1:57, the main melody of the advertised track is removed and the video shows another post, which is actually a text log written by the SiIva A.I., read by the text-to-speech voice "Wiseguy" from Voice Forge, the same voice used for SiIvaGunner. The A.I. reports that the testing on the King for a Day Tournament and Unregistered HyperCam 2's event was successful, and that the quality of his own data stream has improved over the past few months, hypothesizing that the virus within his system had a positive effect on his level of sapience and the sophistication of the parallel universe. The A.I. proposes that he should investigate the virus further, using the greater processing power granted by a recent upgrade to its CPU. He observes that this upgrade resulted in a series of glitches leading to a temporary shutdown in April, during which the channel fell back to the personality model of Gadgetini Clone 2.0, whose parts were used in the construction of the A.I., causing the channel to appear to be taken over by Inspector Gadget. This had an effect on the parallel universe, in which Inspector Gadget took physical form and the Nostalgia Critic of that universe led a rebellion to temporarily gain control of the channel. The A.I. attempts to delete Inspector Gadget from its systems, but fails and simply suppresses the incident. He then decides to conduct a secondary controlled test with a larger control group due to the success of King for a Day, and initiates a "backup antivirus program". The video ends with a third post responding with "take your meds schizo".  ARG[] There are two codes that can be found in the video:  The first code is found in the image name of the last 4chan post. Inputting this string ("-BgZFNlO9TM") as a YouTube video ID leads to the video "Bad Ending - Despicable Me: Minion Rush", which is a dead end. The second code is found via combining the unique IDs of all the 4chan posts ("05210611610", "90780691120", "98089072065") in order. Taking 3 numbers at a time from the combined string produces a series of numbers that can be interpreted as decimal codes for ASCII characters. Inputting the resultant string ("4jtmNEpbYHA") as a YouTube video ID leads to the video "Secret Ending - Despicable Me: Minion Rush", which is needed for the next step in the ARG. Later, in the tournament bracket found in "test" from News Flash! SiIvaGunner: King for a Day Tournament, this log is paired with "Beyond the Gate", focusing on Unregistered HyperCam 2. Throughout the post, there are numbers embedded within some of Unregistered HyperCam 2's dialogue. Taking each number in order of appearance and combining them, similar to the step regarding this log, yields the number "420". This number is then combined with others found within the tournament bracket to lead to the final News Flash! post.;;;minindo

T3zk4TDcXr4;;;Darnell (BF Mix) - Friday Night Funkin';;;https://siivagunner.fandom.com/wiki/Darnell%20(BF%20Mix)%20-%20Friday%20Night%20Funkin' ;;;The rip plays gameplay footage of a modded version of the level with the following changes:  The player character is changed to maddie monotart's "wordle rap / mad mew mew (ny version) song oc". Girlfriend's outfit has been changed to Kyōko's from YuruYuri in reference to "wordle rap"'s visuals. "wordle rap" is mashed up with the advertised track. Much of the vocal melodies are also changed to match "wordle rap". At 1:01 and 1:51, the melody changes to "More One Night" from Girls' Last Tour, referencing "Mad Mew Mew (NY Version) - Undertale". At 1:06, 1:12, 1:18, and 1:25, the flute instrument in the song is changed to "Wordle Rap" by Luke Metcalf. At 1:16, the player-side melody changes to "Baby", referencing "Circus (Heardle Mix) - Five Nights at Freddy's". At 1:20, the melody changes to "Right Foot Creep", referencing "Emote: Griddy - Become Fumo". As is customary with most Friday Night Funkin' rips, the player character dies despite winning the level. During the game over sequenced, the "Retry" shaped brain has been replaced with the Wordle input grid.
